an expert in the sune speciality as the examiner reject

8 . Every candidate for the degree of Master of Surgery 
shall submit to the Registrar, at least one year beforc 
the date fixed for the Part II examination , his application 
for approval of subject of his thesis by the Faculty of 
Medical Sciences, thiough the Head of the Department 
in the teaching institution under whom he proposes to 
work . The Head of the Department shall certify that 
lie will provide the necessary facilities for proper investi 
gations and will supervise the work of the candidate. The 
thesis shall be on a subject connected with the Science 
and Practice of Surgery , excepting subjects which are ex 
clusively Medical and shall be submitted within a period 
of 3 years from the date the subject of the thesis was 
approved , provided that the period may be extended in 
exceptional circumstances . 

The thesis nigy he submitted before or after the exami 
nation . Every candidate shall submit 

(a ) 3 copies of his thesis; 
( b ) 3 copies of the original plan already submitted 

by him at the time of registration ; 
to Registrar, through Head of the Department of the 
teaching institution under whom he has heen permitted 
to work , accompanied by —

XXX 

xxx 
The thesis shall be examined separately by two exami 
ners nominated by Syndicate on the recommendation 
of Faculty . They shall report whether the 
thesis be accepted , or rejected . When the examiners 
disagree , Syndicate shall appoint a third examiner who 
shall be an expert in the same speciality as the examiner 
rejecting the thesis . The third examiner shall be ap 
pointed on recommendation of Faculty of Medical 
Sciences . If the thesis is accepted , it shall be classified 
as accepted , commended or highly commended . 
Syndicate may, on recommendation of Faculty of
